## Overview

Dr. Michael T. Lawton is a neurosurgeon in Phoenix, Arizona and is affiliated with [St. Joseph's Hospital and Medical Center](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/az/st-josephs-hospital-and-medical-center-6860280). He received his medical degree from Johns Hopkins University School of Medicine and has been in practice for more than 20 years. Dr. Michael T. Lawton accepts Aetna, Cigna, Blue Cross, United Healthcare - see other [insurance plans accepted](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/michael-lawton-342227#insurance).

Doctor's Details

Speaks **English**

Works at **St. Joseph's Hospital and Medical Center**

## Specialties

Specialty

### Neurosurgery

Neurosurgeons use minimally invasive surgery to treat brain, nervous system and spinal nerve disorders. Conditions treated by neurosurgeons include brain bleeds, head or spinal cord trauma or tumors.

Subspecialties

### General Neurosurgery

#### Summary of Patient Reviews

Patients generally express high regard for this provider's expertise and dedication to patient well-being. The provider is often commended for clear communication, thoroughness, and a caring manner. However, one patient reported difficulty in receiving sufficient information and expressed dissatisfaction with the surgical outcome.

U.S. News calculates an overall star rating and uses AI to summarizes patient reviews of doctors based on ratings and reviews from across the internet and updates the these periodically to incorporate more recent reviews as they become available. ### What are Dr. Lawton's  areas of care?

Neurosurgeons use minimally invasive surgery to treat brain, nervous system and spinal nerve disorders. Conditions treated by neurosurgeons include brain bleeds, head or spinal cord trauma or tumors.

[profile](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/michael-lawton-342227).
